04609	        The wait for the handle of the thread of the worker is bounded
04610	        by the **current** remaining budget of the round.  It never
04611	        opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of
04612	        seconds.
04613	        """
04614	        worker = _WindowsPipeWorker(self._api, self._handle, kind=kind,
04615	                                    action=action, clock=self._clock,
04616	                                    context=self._context,
04617	                                    name='lvw-native-custody-pipe-' + kind)
04618	        # The pre-registration: the endpoint is the owner of the worker
04619	        # before the thread of the worker exists.
04620	        if kind == 'read':
04621	            self._reader = worker
04622	        else:
04623	            self._writer = worker
04624	        try:
04625	            worker.start()
04626	        except BaseException:
04627	            # The start of the worker did not return.  The worker keeps
04628	            # its owner and it may still come up: it is never forgotten
04629	            # and it is never closed under.
04630	            self._retained.append(worker)
04631	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04632	            self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
04633	            raise
04634	        # The worker acquires the handle of its own thread before it may
04635	        # be used.  The wait for it is bounded by the remaining budget of
04636	        # the round.
04637	        worker.wait_ready(self._remaining(deadline))
04638	        return worker
04639	
04640	    def _await(self, worker, request, deadline, cancel):
04641	        """Wait for the completion of one worker, bounded and cancellable.
04642	
04643	        The **final** admission of the generation happens here: it is
04644	        asked after the worker is ready and it is asked **immediately
04645	        before** the submission of the request, under the lock of the
04646	        admission.  That is the **linearization point** of the request:
04647	        a cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** it
04648	        causes **zero** primitive calls and a cancellation that arrives
04649	        **after** it follows the bounded cancellation below.  No claim
04650	        is made that an operating system call that already began may be
04651	        undone atomically.
04652	
04653	        The cancel flag and the deadline of the call travel with the
04654	        request: the worker asks them itself before it enters the
04655	        operation and a request whose admission was refused never calls
04656	        the primitive.
04657	
04658	        The deadline that this wait really uses is **recomputed on every
04659	        slice**: a deadline that was bound after the wait started still
04660	        tightens the wait that is running.  A wait that carries no
04661	        deadline yet observes the deadline as soon as it is bound, and
04662	        the cancel flag ends it in the meantime.
04663	
04664	        The **result of the generation** wins over the cancellation and
04665	        over a new timeout.  A fault that the worker really published
04666	        before the cancellation of the round is the first error of the
04667	        round: the cancellation of a later close may not replace it and
04668	        it may not turn it into a benign cancellation.  The order of the
04669	        two facts is the order of the events of the worker and it is
04670	        never a guess of the scheduler.
04671	        """
04672	        with self._admission_lock_for():
04673	            # The final admission of the generation and the submission
04674	            # of the request are **one** atomic step.
04675	            effective = self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
04676	            generation = worker.submit(request, cancel=cancel,
04677	                                       deadline=effective)
04678	        while True:
04679	            effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
04680	            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04681	                self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective, cancel)
04682	            if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
04683	                # Before a **new** timeout is decided, the published
04684	                # original error of the **same** generation is
04685	                # inspected: it remains the primary error of the round
04686	                # and the timeout is a **parallel** fact.
04687	                outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04688	                if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04689	                    raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
04690	                try:
04691	                    self._cancel(worker, effective)
04692	                except BrokerError as error:
04693	                    # The coordination failed: the published original
04694	                    # error of the generation is inspected **again**
04695	                    # before the failure of the coordination is
04696	                    # decided, and the failure of the coordination
04697	                    # stays a parallel fact.
04698	                    outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04699	                    if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04700	                        raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome, error)
04701	                    raise
04702	                # Again after the coordination: the outcome of the
04703	                # generation is immutable and it is never rewritten by
04704	                # the timeout.
04705	                outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04706	                if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04707	                    raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
04708	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
04709	            if worker.wait_done(generation, POLL_SLICE_SECONDS):
04710	                break
04711	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04712	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04713	            raise self._outcome_error(outcome)
04714	        if worker.error:
04715	            raise BrokerError(worker.error, self._name)
04716	        return worker.result
04717	
04718	    @staticmethod
04719	    def _parallel_timeout(outcome, parallel=None):
04720	        """The published failure of a generation with the timeout beside it.
04721	
04722	        The **original** error object is the error of the round: a
04723	        timeout that arrives after the failure was really published is
04724	        a **parallel** fact and it never replaces the failure.
04725	        """
04726	        error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
04727	        try:
04728	            error.lvw_parallel_timeout = {
04729	                'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT',
04730	                'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
04731	                'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
04732	                'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
04733	                'coordination_error': (type(parallel).__name__
04734	                                       if parallel is not None else None),
04735	            }
04736	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04737	            pass
04738	        return error
04739	
04740	    @staticmethod
04741	    def _outcome_error(outcome):
04742	        """The error of one published outcome of a worker.
04743	
04744	        The **original** error object is raised when the worker kept
04745	        it: the object is the failure and the code is only its name.  A
04746	        worker that kept only a code raises the stable code of the
04747	        broker.
04748	        """
04749	        original = outcome.get('error_object')
04750	        if original is not None:
04751	            return original
04752	        return BrokerError(outcome.get('error'), 'WORKER_OUTCOME')
04753	
04754	    def _raise_cancellation(self, worker, generation, effective, cancel):
04755	        """Raise the cancellation of this generation of the worker.
04756	
04757	        The outcome of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
04758	        cancelled and it is asked again after the worker really stopped.
04759	        A failure of this generation is the **first error** of the
04760	        round: a cancellation that arrives afterwards may never replace
04761	        it and it may never turn it into a benign cancellation.  The
04762	        cancellation stays a **parallel** fact and it is named next to
04763	        the failure.
04764	
04765	        A **failed** coordination - a cancellation that could not be
04766	        confirmed or a join that did not end - is a parallel fact as
04767	        well: it never replaces the published failure of the
04768	        generation and it never hides it.
04769	
04770	        The error of the cancellation carries the owner of the flag: the
04771	        origin of the cancellation is named at the place where the error
04772	        is really raised and never by a caller of it.
04773	        """
04774	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04775	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04776	            raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel)
04777	        coordination_error = None
04778	        try:
04779	            self._cancel(worker, effective)
04780	        except BrokerError as error:
04781	            coordination_error = error
04782	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04783	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
04784	            raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error)
04785	        if coordination_error is not None:
04786	            raise coordination_error
04787	        raise _cancel_source_error(
04788	            BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
04789	
04790	    @staticmethod
04791	    def _parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error=None):
04792	        """The failure of a generation with the cancellation beside it.
04793	
04794	        The failure is the error of the round.  The cancellation that
04795	        was requested at the same moment is a **parallel** fact: it is
04796	        named next to the failure and it never replaces it.  A
04797	        coordination that failed is a parallel fact as well.
04798	        """
04799	        error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
04800	        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04801	            source = None
04802	            owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
04803	            if callable(owner):
04804	                try:
04805	                    source = getattr(owner(), 'owner', None)
04806	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04807	                    source = None
04808	            try:
04809	                error.lvw_parallel_cancellation = {
04810	                    'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
04811	                    'source': source,
04812	                    'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
04813	                    'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
04814	                    'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
04815	                    'coordination_error': (type(coordination_error).__name__
04816	                                           if coordination_error is not None
04817	                                           else None),
04818	                }
04819	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04820	                pass
04821	        return error
04822	
04823	    def _cancel(self, worker, deadline=None):
04824	        """Cancel one worker and prove that it really stopped."""
04825	        state = worker.request_cancel(self._effective_deadline(deadline))
04826	        if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
04827	            # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner reference and
04828	            # the round becomes unknown.  It is never terminated blindly
04829	            # and it is never waited for without a bound.
04830	            self._retained.append(worker)
04831	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04832	            self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
04833	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED',
04834	                              '%s:%s' % (self._name, state))
04835	        if not worker.joined:
04836	            self._retained.append(worker)
04837	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04838	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED', self._name)
04839	
04840	    def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
04841	        """Read one block of the pipe, bounded by the deadline.
04842	
04843	        The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
04844	        started and before the request is submitted: a read that enters
04845	        when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
04846	        **no** primitive call at all.
04847	        """
04848	        effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
04849	        if self._reader is None:
04850	            self._start('read', self._read_block, effective)
04851	        return self._await(self._reader, None, deadline, cancel)
04852	
04853	    def write(self, data, deadline=None, cancel=None):
04854	        """Write the whole block, bounded by the deadline.
04855	
04856	        The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
04857	        started and before the request is submitted: a write that enters
04858	        when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
04859	        **no** primitive call at all.
04860	        """
04861	        effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
04862	        if self._writer is None:
04863	            self._start('write', self._write_block, effective)
04864	        # The cancel flag and the deadline of this write are published
04865	        # for the partial write: every segment of a partial write checks
04866	        # the cancel flag and the deadline of the round.  They are not
04867	        # cleared when the write ends: a segment that is still in flight
04868	        # inside the worker has to observe the cancel of this write.
04869	        self._write_cancel = cancel
04870	        self._write_deadline = effective
04871	        return self._await(self._writer, bytes(data), deadline, cancel)
04872	
04873	    def _read_block(self, request):
04874	        return self._api.read_stream(self._handle, PIPE_READ_BYTES)
04875	
04876	    def _current_write_deadline(self):
04877	        """The deadline that the next segment of a write really uses.
04878	
04879	        The value is read **again** for every segment of a partial
04880	        write.  A copy of the deadline that was taken when the write
04881	        started would let the write continue past a deadline that the
04882	        round published in the meantime, and the shared context of the
04883	        round is the only authority of the deadline.
04884	
04885	        The **published cleanup deadline** is part of it: a write that
04886	        is still running when the round entered its cleanup is bounded by
04887	        the cleanup budget of the round and not by the long lease.
04888	        """
04889	        value = self._write_deadline
04890	        bound = self._deadline
04891	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
04892	            value = bound
04893	        context = self._context
04894	        if context is not None:
04895	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
04896	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
04897	                value = absolute
04898	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
04899	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
04900	                value = cleanup
04901	        return value
04902	
04903	    def _write_block(self, request):
04904	        """Write the whole block, report the number of the written bytes.
04905	
04906	        Windows may write less than the block that it was given.  The
04907	        endpoint repeats the write until the whole block was written or
04908	        until the write fails with a named error.  **Every segment** of
04909	        the partial write checks the cancel flag and the deadline of the
04910	        round: a write that is not bounded is not a write.
04911	        """
04912	        data = bytes(request)
04913	        total = 0
04914	        while total < len(data):
04915	            cancel = self._write_cancel
04916	            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
04917	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
04918	            deadline = self._current_write_deadline()
04919	            if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
04920	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
04921	            count = self._api.write_stream(self._handle, data[total:])
04922	            if count is None or count <= 0:
04923	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
04924	                                  '%s:written=%d' % (self._name, total))
04925	            total += count
04926	        return total
04927	
04928	    def close(self):
04929	        """Close the original handle of this endpoint, exactly once.
04930	
04931	        The close stops the workers from accepting a new task first, it
04932	        asks for the cancellation of the operation that is in flight and
04933	        it joins the workers with a bounded wait.  A worker that did not
04934	        stop keeps its owner reference: the file is never closed under a
04935	        worker that is still inside its operation.
04936	
04937	        The close is **committed** only when it really happened.  A
04938	        close that failed keeps the file and its owner: the endpoint
04939	        may try it again inside the deadline of the round and it is
04940	        never marked as closed irreversibly.  A close of an endpoint
04941	        that is already closed keeps the fact that it is closed: the
04942	        second close is idempotent and it never turns a proven close
04943	        into a missing one.
04944	
04945	        The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
04946	        worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
04947	        released is not retained any more, and a worker whose handle of
04948	        its thread could not be closed is retained again and it may be
04949	        released by a later close.  A closed file never locks the
04950	        recovery of the handle of a thread.
04951	        """
04952	        deadline = self._effective_deadline()
04953	        alive = False
04954	        retained = []
04955	        for worker in (self._reader, self._writer):
04956	            if worker is None:
04957	                continue
04958	            worker.stop_accepting()
04959	            worker.request_cancel(deadline)
04960	            if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
04961	                # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner and the
04962	                # file is never closed under it.
04963	                alive = True
04964	                retained.append(worker)
04965	                continue
04966	            # The thread of the worker really ended: the original handle
04967	            # of the thread may be released now.  A handle that could
04968	            # not be closed keeps its owner and a later close may try it
04969	            # again.
04970	            if not worker.release_thread_handle():
04971	                retained.append(worker)
04972	        # The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
04973	        # worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
04974	        # released is not retained any more.  A closed file never locks
04975	        # the recovery of the handle of a thread.
04976	        self._retained = retained
04977	        self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
04978	        self.facts['worker_alive'] = alive
04979	        self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = all(
04980	            worker.thread_handle_released
04981	            for worker in (self._reader, self._writer) if worker is not None)
04982	        if alive:
04983	            # A live worker keeps the file: the file is never closed
04984	            # under a worker that is still inside its operation.
04985	            self.facts['closed'] = False
04986	            return self.facts
04987	        if self._closed:
04988	            self.facts['closed'] = True
04989	            self.facts['already_closed'] = True
04990	            return self.facts
04991	        try:
04992	            self._file.close()
04993	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04994	            self.facts['closed'] = False
04995	            self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
04996	            return self.facts
04997	        self._closed = True
04998	        self.facts['closed'] = True
04999	        return self.facts
05000	
05001	
05002	def _make_endpoint(file_object, name, *, api=None, clock=None, context=None):
05003	    """The endpoint of one end of the private pipe of this host.
05004	
05005	    The Windows host gets the supervised and cancellable endpoint and
05006	    every other host gets the non blocking endpoint of the original
05007	    descriptor.  The choice is made by the host and never by a string
05008	    that a caller could set: a platform that is not Windows never loads
05009	    the Windows table.
05010	
05011	    The Windows endpoint needs the primitive table of the host, because
05012	    the cancellation of a synchronous operation needs the original
05013	    handle of the thread that performs it.  The table is loaded only on
05014	    Windows.
05015	
05016	    The ONE context of the round is handed to the endpoint: the
05017	    endpoint reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it
05018	    never keeps a budget of its own.  The context is bound to the
05019	    **primitive table** as well when the endpoint owns the table: a
05020	    table that does not know the context of the round would open a
05021	    budget of its own.
05022	    """
05023	    if os.name == 'nt':
05024	        if api is None:
05025	            api = WindowsApi()
05026	        binder = getattr(api, 'bind_context', None)
05027	        if callable(binder) and context is not None:
05028	            try:
05029	                binder(context)
05030	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05031	                pass
05032	        return _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, api=api, clock=clock,
05033	                                    context=context)
05034	    return _PosixPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, clock=clock)
05035	
05036	
05037	# -- the private identity of a cancellation of the round --------------------
05038	
05039	class _CloseSource:
05040	    """The private identity of one close of one channel.
05041	
05042	    The identity is an **object**: two closes of the same channel never
05043	    share it and the owner of a cancellation is compared by identity
05044	    and never by the name of a code.  The sequence is the order of the
05045	    closes of the channel and it is part of the evidence.
05046	    """
05047	
05048	    def __init__(self, sequence):
05049	        self.sequence = sequence
05050	        self.owner = 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'
05051	
05052	
05053	class _ChannelCancel:
05054	    """The cancel flag of one channel, with the owner that set it.
05055	
05056	    The flag names the **owner** that set it: a cancellation that the
05057	    close of this channel initiated is provable and a cancellation that
05058	    another owner initiated is not.  The owner is registered in the
05059	    same atomic step as the setting of the flag and it is registered
05060	    **before** the flag is set, so that a reader can never see the flag
05061	    set without its owner.
05062	
05063	    The **first** owner wins: a flag that another owner already set is
05064	    never re-labelled by a later one.  A cancellation that this channel
05065	    did not initiate is therefore never taken for a cancellation of the
05066	    close of this channel.
05067	    """
05068	
05069	    def __init__(self):
05070	        self._event = threading.Event()
05071	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
05072	        self._owner = None
05073	
05074	    def is_set(self):
05075	        return self._event.is_set()
05076	
05077	    def set(self, owner=None):
05078	        """Set the flag and name the owner that set it.
05079	
05080	        The owner is written under the lock of the flag and it is
05081	        written **before** the flag is set: the registration of the
05082	        owner is never later than the cancellation itself.
05083	        """
05084	        with self._lock:
05085	            if not self._event.is_set():
05086	                if owner is not None:
05087	                    self._owner = owner
05088	                self._event.set()
05089	
05090	    def owner(self):
05091	        """The owner that set the flag, or None when it named none."""
05092	        with self._lock:
05093	            return self._owner
05094	
05095	    def wait(self, timeout=None):
05096	        return self._event.wait(timeout)
05097	
05098	
05099	def _cancel_source_error(error, cancel):
05100	    """Stamp the owner of a cancel flag on the error of a cancellation.
05101	
05102	    The owner is read from the flag **at the origin of the error**: the
05103	    error itself carries the identity of the owner that really set the
05104	    flag and it never carries a claim of a caller.  A flag that names
05105	    no owner leaves the error untouched: an unknown source is never
05106	    taken for a known one.
05107	    """
05108	    owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
05109	    if not callable(owner):
05110	        return error
05111	    try:
05112	        source = owner()
05113	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05114	        return error
05115	    if source is None:
05116	        return error
05117	    try:
05118	        error.lvw_cancel_source = source
05119	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05120	        pass
05121	    return error
05122	
05123	
05124	# -- the private control channel -------------------------------------------
05125	
05126	class StreamChannel:
05127	    """The private control channel of the broker.
05128	
05129	    The channel is the broker's own standard input and output.  It is
05130	    bounded three times: one frame is at most 32 KiB, the channel carries
05131	    at most 1024 frames and the text of the children is bounded by the
05132	    core.
05133	
05134	    The read side is drained by one background thread, so the broker never
05135	    blocks on the read.  A parent that stops talking therefore cannot hold
05136	    the deadline of the round: the deadline is checked by the broker
05137	    itself.
05138	
05139	    The channel owns the original handles of its two ends.  A read and a
05140	    write are bounded by an absolute deadline and by a cancel flag, so a
05141	    peer that never reads can never hold the supervisor of the round.
05142	    """
05143	
05144	    def __init__(self, reader, writer, *, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES,
05145	                 max_frame_bytes=MAX_FRAME_BYTES, name='channel',
05146	                 read_endpoint=None, write_endpoint=None, clock=None, api=None,
05147	                 context=None, custody=None):
05148	        self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=max_frames)
05149	        self._max_frames = max_frames
05150	        self._max_frame_bytes = max_frame_bytes
05151	        self._count = 0
05152	        self._overflow = False
05153	        self._overflow_facts = {}
05154	        self._eof = False
05155	        self._error = None
05156	        self._buffer = b''
05157	        self._deadline = None
05158	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
05159	        # The cancel flag of the channel names the owner that set it:
05160	        # only a cancellation that the close of **this** channel
05161	        # initiated is provable and only a provable one is a normal
05162	        # fact of the close.
05163	        self._cancel = _ChannelCancel()
05164	        # The private state of the close of this channel: the identity
05165	        # of the close that is running now and the named fact of the
05166	        # cancellation that this close really initiated.
05167	        self._close_source = None
05168	        self._close_sequence = 0
05169	        self._close_cancellation = None
05170	        # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
05171	        # failure of the reader: it is a parallel fact and it never
05172	        # replaces the failure.
05173	        self._parallel_cancellation = None
05174	        self.reader_alive = None
05175	        self.reader_finished = None
05176	        # The ONE context of the round.  The channel and its endpoints
05177	        # read the absolute deadline of the round from it and they never
05178	        # keep a budget of their own.
05179	        self._context = context
05180	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drain, daemon=True,
05181	                                        name='lvw-native-custody-channel')
05182	        # The channel registers itself with the custody of the launcher
05183	        # **before** it builds anything: a channel whose construction
05184	        # failed half way - an endpoint that could not be built, a
05185	        # reader thread that did not start - may not leave an endpoint
05186	        # and a thread without an owner.  The outer owner of the launch
05187	        # finds the partial channel through this registration and it
05188	        # never closes the files of the process by itself.
05189	        self._custody = custody
05190	        if custody is not None:
05191	            custody.retain('channel', self, 'constructing')
05192	        try:
05193	            self._reader = read_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
05194	                reader, name + '-read', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
05195	            # The parameter of the constructor is ``write_endpoint`` and
05196	            # the attribute of the channel is ``_writer``.  The two names
05197	            # are never mixed: a name that does not exist raises a
05198	            # ``NameError`` and every construction of a channel of this
05199	            # package fails with it.
05200	            self._writer = write_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
05201	                writer, name + '-write', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
05202	            self._thread.start()
05203	        except BaseException:
05204	            # The construction of the channel did not return: the
05205	            # channel keeps its owner in the custody of the launcher and
05206	            # it is never forgotten.
05207	            raise
05208	
05209	    def bind_context(self, context):
05210	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to the channel.
05211	
05212	        The context is bound to the channel **and** to its two
05213	        endpoints: every layer of the round reads the same absolute
05214	        deadline and none of them keeps a deadline of its own.
05215	        """
05216	        if context is not None:
05217	            self._context = context
05218	            for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
05219	                binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_context', None)
05220	                if callable(binder):
05221	                    binder(context)
05222	        return self._context
05223	
05224	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
05225	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the channel.
05226	
05227	        The deadline is bound to the channel **and** to its two
05228	        endpoints.  A binding that happens after a read started still
05229	        tightens that read: the deadline is a property of the endpoint
05230	        and not a local argument of one call.
05231	        """
05232	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
05233	        if value is None:
05234	            return self._deadline
05235	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
05236	            return self._deadline
05237	        self._deadline = value
05238	        for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
05239	            binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_deadline', None)
05240	            if callable(binder):
05241	                try:
05242	                    binder(value)
05243	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05244	                    pass
05245	        return value
05246	
05247	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
05248	        """The ONE deadline that a call of this channel really uses.
05249	
05250	        The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
05251	        channel, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
05252	        cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of the
05253	        four.
05254	
05255	        The published cleanup deadline is part of it.  A round that
05256	        entered its cleanup publishes one deadline, and the transport of
05257	        the round - the write of the terminal reply and the close of the
05258	        channel - is bound by it: a transport that still waited with the
05259	        long lease of the round would outlive the only cleanup budget
05260	        of the round.
05261	        """
05262	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
05263	        bound = self._deadline
05264	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
05265	            value = bound
05266	        context = self._context
05267	        if context is not None:
05268	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
05269	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
05270	                value = absolute
05271	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
05272	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
05273	                value = cleanup
05274	        return value
05275	
05276	    def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
05277	        """The seconds that are left until the deadline of the channel."""
05278	        value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
05279	        if value is None:
05280	            return None
05281	        return max(0.0, value - (self._clock or time.monotonic)())
05282	
05283	    def _drain(self):
05284	        try:
05285	            while True:
05286	                block = self._reader.read(self._deadline, self._cancel)
05287	                if not block:
05288	                    break
05289	                buffer = self._buffer + block
05290	                self._buffer = b''
05291	                while True:
05292	                    index = buffer.find(b'\n')
05293	                    if index < 0:
05294	                        break
05295	                    raw = buffer[:index + 1]
05296	                    buffer = buffer[index + 1:]
05297	                    if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
05298	                        self._record_overflow('FRAME_TOO_LARGE',
05299	                                              {'frame_bytes': len(raw),
05300	                                               'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
05301	                        return
05302	                    self._count += 1
05303	                    if self._count > self._max_frames:
05304	                        self._record_overflow('TOO_MANY_FRAMES',
05305	                                              {'frames': self._count,
05306	                                               'limit': self._max_frames})
05307	                        return
05308	                    while True:
05309	                        self._check_wait()
05310	                        try:
05311	                            self._queue.put(raw, timeout=0.5)
05312	                            break
05313	                        except queue.Full:
05314	                            if self._eof:
05315	                                return
05316	                            continue
05317	                # The remainder of the block has no newline.  The buffer
05318	                # of the channel is bounded: it is checked on **every**
05319	                # append and an oversize buffer is refused at once.  One
05320	                # byte beyond the limit is kept, so that the overflow is
05321	                # detected and not silently trimmed.
05322	                if len(buffer) > self._max_frame_bytes:
05323	                    self._buffer = buffer[:self._max_frame_bytes + 1]
05324	                    self._record_overflow('BUFFER_TOO_LARGE',
05325	                                          {'buffer_bytes': len(self._buffer),
05326	                                           'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
05327	                    return
05328	                self._buffer = buffer
05329	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05330	            self._record_reader_error(error)
05331	        finally:
05332	            self._eof = True
05333	            if self._buffer and self._error is None:
05334	                # The stream ended in the middle of a frame.  An
05335	                # incomplete frame is a named failure of the channel and
05336	                # it is never dropped silently.
05337	                self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME'
05338	
05339	    def _record_overflow(self, reason, facts):
05340	        """Record the overflow of the channel at the place where it happened.
05341	
05342	        The overflow is a **named** failure of the channel and it is
05343	        recorded **here**, where it really happened.  A flag that only
05344	        the internals of the channel know would leave the close of the
05345	        channel with no error at all: the reader of a channel that
05346	        overflowed would look like a reader that ended normally and the
05347	        round would release its resources on a fact that never happened.
05348	
05349	        The error of the channel is **sticky**: the first named failure
05350	        of the reader stays the error of the channel and the overflow
05351	        never replaces it.
05352	        """
05353	        self._overflow = True
05354	        self._overflow_facts = dict(facts, reason=reason)
05355	        if self._error is None:
05356	            self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW'
05357	
05358	    def _record_reader_error(self, error):
05359	        """Record the error of the reader of this channel.
05360	
05361	        A cancellation that **this** channel initiated with **this**
05362	        close is not a failure of the reader: it is a named fact of the
05363	        close.  It is kept with its code, with its source and with its
05364	        order for the evidence, and it never replaces the error of the
05365	        reader and it never clears a historical error of the reader.
05366	
05367	        Every other error keeps the original error of the reader and the
05368	        round stays unknown: an ordinary failure of the read, a timeout,
05369	        an overflow, a partial frame, an error that was already there
05370	        before the close and a cancellation whose owner is unknown or
05371	        is not the close of this channel.
05372	        """
05373	        source = getattr(error, 'lvw_cancel_source', None)
05374	        if source is not None and source is self._close_source:
05375	            # This cancellation was initiated by the close of this
05376	            # channel: it is a named fact of the close and it is not a
05377	            # failure of the reader.  The historical error of the reader
05378	            # is never cleared and it is never replaced by this fact.
05379	            self._close_cancellation = {
05380	                'code': getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__,
05381	                'source': getattr(source, 'owner', 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'),
05382	                'sequence': getattr(source, 'sequence', None),
05383	                'error_type': type(error).__name__,
05384	            }
05385	            return
05386	        parallel = getattr(error, 'lvw_parallel_cancellation', None)
05387	        if parallel is not None:
05388	            # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
05389	            # failure of the reader is a **parallel** fact: it is named
05390	            # next to the failure and it never replaces it.
05391	            self._parallel_cancellation = dict(parallel)
05392	        self._error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
05393	
05394	    def _check_wait(self):
05395	        """The cancel flag and the deadline of the wait of the reader."""
05396	        if self._cancel.is_set():
05397	            # The error carries the owner of the flag: the reader of
05398	            # the channel and the close of the channel agree on the
05399	            # origin of the cancellation and the evidence can prove it.
05400	            raise _cancel_source_error(BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_CANCELLED'),
05401	                                       self._cancel)
05402	        remaining = self._remaining()
05403	        if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
05404	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT')
05405	
05406	    def read(self, timeout):
05407	        """One raw frame, ``None`` when nothing arrived, ``b''`` at the end."""
05408	        if self._overflow:
05409	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW')
05410	        try:
05411	            return self._queue.get(timeout=max(0.0, timeout))
05412	        except queue.Empty:
05413	            if self._error:
05414	                raise BrokerError(self._error)
05415	            if self._eof:
05416	                return b''
05417	            return None
05418	
05419	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
05420	        """Write one frame, bounded by the deadline of the round.
05421	
05422	        The number of the bytes that really went into the pipe is
05423	        checked: a partial write is never reported as a success.
05424	        """
05425	        if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
05426	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
05427	        target = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
05428	        written = self._writer.write(raw, target, self._cancel)
05429	        if written is not None and written != len(raw):
05430	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_WRITE_INCOMPLETE',
05431	                              'written=%s:expected=%d' % (written, len(raw)))
05432	        return written
05433	
05434	    def close(self):
05435	        """Close the channel and report whether the reader really stopped.
05436	
05437	        The reader thread is joined with a bounded wait.  A reader that
05438	        did not stop is reported as such: a daemon thread is not a
05439	        finished thread, so the fact that the reader is still alive is
05440	        kept visible instead of being hidden behind the close.  The read
05441	        end is closed by its owner only when the reader really stopped.
05442	
05443	        The facts of the close carry the error of the reader, the number
05444	        of the workers that the endpoints retained and the fact that the
05445	        original handles of those workers were released: a reader that
05446	        ended with an error is not a successful end.
05447	
05448	        The close may be **repeated**: a close that did not confirm its
05449	        ends may try them again, and a close of an endpoint that is
05450	        already closed keeps the fact that it is closed.
05451	
05452	        The error of the reader of the channel is **sticky** and it is
05453	        never cleared by a later close of the channel: a close that
05454	        returned is not a release and the error of the reader stays.
05455	
05456	        The close **initiates** the cancellation of the reader and it
05457	        names itself as the owner of that cancellation before the flag
05458	        is set.  A cancellation that this close really initiated is a
05459	        named fact of the close and it is not a failure of the reader; a
05460	        cancellation of another owner, a failure of the read that
05461	        happened before the close and every other error of the reader
05462	        keep the round unknown.
05463	        """
05464	        facts = {}
05465	        # The identity of this close is published **before** the
05466	        # cancellation is initiated and the flag registers the owner in
05467	        # the same atomic step as the setting of the flag.
05468	        self._close_sequence += 1
05469	        source = _CloseSource(self._close_sequence)
05470	        self._close_source = source
05471	        self._cancel.set(source)
05472	        try:
05473	            write_facts = self._writer.close()
05474	            facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(write_facts.get('closed'))
05475	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = int(
05476	                write_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
05477	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05478	                write_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
05479	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05480	            facts['write_end_closed'] = False
05481	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = 0
05482	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
05483	        remaining = self._remaining()
05484	        join_seconds = CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS if remaining is None else remaining
05485	        start_unconfirmed = False
05486	        try:
05487	            self._thread.join(join_seconds)
05488	        except RuntimeError:
05489	            # The join could not prove the end of the reader: the start
05490	            # of the thread of the reader was **interrupted** or it was
05491	            # never confirmed.  A start that did not return may still
05492	            # come up and a late reader may still read from the read
05493	            # end of the channel.  The state of the reader therefore
05494	            # stays **unknown**: the read end is never closed underneath
05495	            # a possibly late target and the round keeps the fact.
05496	            self.reader_alive = True
05497	            self.reader_finished = False
05498	            start_unconfirmed = True
05499	        else:
05500	            self.reader_alive = self._thread.is_alive()
05501	            self.reader_finished = not self.reader_alive
05502	        facts['reader_finished'] = self.reader_finished
05503	        facts['reader_error'] = self._error
05504	        # The start of the reader that was never confirmed is a named
05505	        # fact of the close: the state of the reader is unknown and it
05506	        # is never read as a finished reader.
05507	        facts['reader_start_unconfirmed'] = start_unconfirmed
05508	        # The facts of the overflow are published with the close: the
05509	        # overflow is a named failure of the channel and it is never
05510	        # only an internal flag.
05511	        facts['overflow'] = bool(self._overflow)
05512	        facts['overflow_facts'] = dict(self._overflow_facts)
05513	        # The parallel fact of the cancellation of the close: it never
05514	        # replaces the error of the reader.
05515	        facts['reader_parallel_cancellation'] = (
05516	            dict(self._parallel_cancellation)
05517	            if self._parallel_cancellation else None)
05518	        # The named fact of the cancellation that this close really
05519	        # initiated.  It is **not** the error of the reader and it does
05520	        # not prove that the reader, the thread, the worker or the
05521	        # handle of the round were released: the strict facts of the
05522	        # close above are the ones that prove it.
05523	        facts['close_cancellation'] = (dict(self._close_cancellation)
05524	                                       if self._close_cancellation else None)
05525	        if self.reader_alive:
05526	            # The reader is still inside the pipe: the read end is not
05527	            # closed under it.  It stays owned by the channel and the
05528	            # round keeps the fact.  The facts of the reader endpoint are
05529	            # the real ones and they are never replaced by a zero.
05530	            endpoint_facts = getattr(self._reader, 'facts', None)
05531	            endpoint_facts = endpoint_facts if isinstance(endpoint_facts, dict) else {}
05532	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
05533	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
05534	                endpoint_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
05535	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05536	                endpoint_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
05537	            facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05538	                facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
05539	                and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
05540	            facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
05541	                                                  + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
05542	            return facts
05543	        try:
05544	            read_facts = self._reader.close()
05545	            facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(read_facts.get('closed'))
05546	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
05547	                read_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
05548	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05549	                read_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
05550	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05551	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
05552	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = 0
05553	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
05554	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
05555	            facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
05556	            and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
05557	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
05558	                                              + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
05559	        return facts
05560	
05561	    @property
05562	    def eof(self):
05563	        return self._eof
05564	
05565	    @property
05566	    def overflow(self):
05567	        return self._overflow
05568	
05569	    @property
05570	    def frames(self):
05571	        return self._count
05572	
05573	
05574	# -- the bounded command loop ----------------------------------------------
05575	
05576	class _StartupOwner:
05577	    """The bounded owner of one startup step of the round.
05578	
05579	    A startup step may block: the creation of a job, the creation of a
05580	    process and the assignment of a process may all block inside the
05581	    operating system.  Such a step may not run unbounded inside the one
05582	    supervision thread of the round.
05583	
05584	    The owner therefore runs the step in its own thread and hands the
05585	    result over through a **bounded** channel of one slot.  The
05586	    supervisor waits for the result until the absolute deadline of the
05587	    round.  A step that did not finish in time keeps the round unknown,
05588	    keeps the thread and its owner, and a result that arrives late may
05589	    only clean up: it may never continue the startup of the round.
05590	    """
05591	
05592	    def __init__(self, *, name, clock, deadline):
05593	        self.name = name
05594	        self._clock = clock
05595	        self._deadline = deadline
05596	        self._results = queue.Queue(maxsize=STARTUP_INFLIGHT_SLOTS)
05597	        self._thread = None
05598	        self._error = None
05599	        self._value = None
05600	        self._finished = threading.Event()
05601	        self._expired = False
05602	        # Whether the start of the thread of this owner **returned**.
05603	        # A start that did not return may still come up: the owner keeps
05604	        # its thread and the round keeps the fact.
05605	        self._started = False
05606	
05607	    def start(self, action):
05608	        """Run one startup step in the thread of this owner.
05609	
05610	        The start of the thread is **one** step and it is recorded: a
05611	        start that did not return keeps its owner and the thread of the
05612	        step may still come up.  The owner is never released because
05613	        the start failed.
05614	        """
05615	        self._thread = threading.Thread(
05616	            target=self._run, args=(action,), daemon=True,
05617	            name='lvw-native-custody-startup-' + self.name)
05618	        self._thread.start()
05619	        self._started = True
05620	        return self
05621	
05622	    def _run(self, action):
05623	        try:
05624	            value = action()
05625	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05626	            self._error = error
05627	            outcome = ('error', error)
05628	        else:
05629	            self._value = value
05630	            outcome = ('value', value)
05631	        # The **attempt** of the step ended.  The event of the attempt is
05632	        # published here and it is never a proof that the thread of the
05633	        # step ended: the thread still has to publish its result and to
05634	        # leave.
05635	        self._finished.set()
05636	        self._put(outcome)
05637	
05638	    def _put(self, item):
05639	        try:
05640	            self._results.put_nowait(item)
05641	        except queue.Full:
05642	            # The bounded channel is full: the result is dropped and the
05643	            # owner keeps the error.  The channel never grows.
05644	            pass
05645	
05646	    def await_result(self):
05647	        """Wait for the result of the step, bounded by the deadline."""
05648	        while True:
05649	            if self._clock() >= self._deadline:
05650	                self._expired = True
05651	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', self.name)
05652	            try:
05653	                kind, value = self._results.get(timeout=POLL_SLICE_SECONDS)
05654	            except queue.Empty:
05655	                continue
05656	            if kind == 'error':
05657	                raise value
05658	            return value
05659	
05660	    def settle(self, seconds):
05661	        """Wait, bounded, for the **thread** of this owner to end.
05662	
05663	        The wait is bounded by the shared cleanup deadline of the round
05664	        and it never adds a settle budget of its own.
05665	
05666	        The end of the **attempt** of the step and the end of the
05667	        **thread** that performs it are two different facts.  The event
05668	        of the attempt is published before the result and before the
05669	        thread leaves, and it is therefore never a proof that the
05670	        thread ended.  The settle asks the thread itself: a thread that
05671	        is still running keeps its owner and the round stays unknown.
05672	
05673	        A **start that did not return** is unknown as well: the thread
05674	        of the step may still come up and the owner is never released
05675	        because the attempt ended.
05676	        """
05677	        if self._thread is None:
05678	            return True
05679	        if not self._started:
05680	            # The start of the thread did not return: the thread may
05681	            # still come up and the owner is never released.
05682	            return False
05683	        try:
05684	            self._thread.join(max(0.0, seconds))
05685	        except RuntimeError:
05686	            # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
05687	            # round that ended.
05688	            return False
05689	        return not self._thread.is_alive()
05690	
05691	    @property
05692	    def expired(self):
05693	        """Whether the step of this owner missed the deadline."""
05694	        return self._expired
05695	
05696	    @property
05697	    def in_flight(self):
05698	        """Whether the step of this owner is still running."""
05699	        return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive()
05700	
05701	
05702	class BrokerLoop:
05703	    """The bounded command loop of the broker.
05704	
05705	    The loop owns the absolute deadline of the round.  It is fixed once,
05706	    from the lease of the plan, and it is never moved: neither a status
05707	    request nor a heartbeat may extend it.
05708	
05709	    The round is stopped when the parent asks for it, when the channel
05710	    reaches its end, when a bound of the channel or of the text of the
05711	    children is exceeded or when the absolute deadline arrives.  A stop
05712	    that was not requested by the parent keeps the evidence of the round
05713	    unknown: the round did not end in a proven release.
05714	
05715	    The startup of the round is supervised.  The arm and the launch are
05716	    **bounded startup steps** with their own owner: a step that blocks
05717	    may not hold the supervision of the round and a step that returns
05718	    after the absolute deadline may only clean up.
05719	    """
05720	
05721	    def __init__(self, *, core, transport, plan, nonce, clock=None, wall=None,
05722	                 max_frames=MAX_FRAMES):
05723	        self.core = core
05724	        self.transport = transport
05725	        self.plan = plan
05726	        self.nonce = nonce
05727	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
05728	        self._wall = wall or time.time
05729	        self._max_frames = max_frames
05730	        self._sequence = 0
05731	        self._last_sequence = None
05732	        self._frames = 0
05733	        self._sent_frames = 0
05734	        self._deadline_wall = None
05735	        self._deadline_mono = None
05736	        self._last_frame_wall = None
05737	        self._last_alive_wall = None
05738	        self._stopped = False
05739	        # The ONE context of the round.  The loop holds the very same
05740	        # object as the core: the deadlines, the cancel and the unknown
05741	        # of the round are one state and not one number per layer.
05742	        self.context = core.context
05743	        # The owner of the startup step that is in flight.  A step that
05744	        # did not end keeps the round unknown and it keeps its owner.
05745	        self._startup_owner = None
05746	        self._startup_in_flight = None
05747	
05748	    # -- the deadline ------------------------------------------------------
05749	    def fix_deadline(self):
05750	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round and return it.
05751	
05752	        The deadline is fixed **before** the transport of the round
05753	        exists: the reader of the channel and the workers of the
05754	        endpoints never start without a deadline and the child has no
05755	        unbound window.
05756	        """
05757	        return self._fix_deadline()
05758	
05759	    def bind_transport(self, transport):
05760	        """Bind the transport of the round to this loop.
05761	
05762	        The transport is bound after the deadline of the round was
05763	        fixed: the transport of the round is never created without the
05764	        deadline that bounds it.
05765	        """
05766	        self.transport = transport
05767	        return transport
05768	
05769	    def _fix_deadline(self):
05770	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round, once, before anything.
05771	
05772	        The deadline is fixed before the job of the round is created,
05773	        before the first reply is written and before the first role is
05774	        launched.  It is the earlier of the lease of the plan and the
05775	        runtime of the plan and it is never moved afterwards.
05776	        """
05777	        if self._deadline_mono is not None:
05778	            return self._deadline_mono
05779	        wall_now = self._wall()
05780	        self._deadline_wall = min(self.plan.lease_expires_at,
05781	                                  wall_now + self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
05782	        self._deadline_mono = self._clock() + max(0.0, self._deadline_wall - wall_now)
05783	        return self._deadline_mono
05784	
05785	    def remaining_seconds(self):
05786	        if self._deadline_mono is None:
05787	            return float(self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
05788	        return max(0.0, self._deadline_mono - self._clock())
05789	
05790	    # -- the replies -------------------------------------------------------
05791	    def _check_frame_budget(self, reserved=RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES):
05792	        """The frame budget of the channel, in both directions.
05793	
05794	        The budget of the round is shared by the frames that the broker
05795	        sends and by the frames that it receives: the round of six hours
05796	        with its heartbeat stays inside the budget of the channel.  The
05797	        frames of the terminal exchange are reserved and the budget is
05798	        never widened and never renewed.
05799	
05800	        The check is the **total** of both directions.  A single
05801	        direction is never a substitute for the total: a channel that
05802	        received a thousand frames and sent none is not inside the
05803	        budget when the budget is a thousand and twenty four frames.
05804	        """
05805	        if self._frames + self._sent_frames + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
05806	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED',
05807	                              'sent=%d:received=%d' % (self._sent_frames,
05808	                                                       self._frames))
05809	
05810	    def _reply(self, kind, state, detail, *, terminal=False):
05811	        self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
05812	        self._sent_frames += 1
05813	        self._sequence += 1
05814	        frame = {'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA, 'kind': kind, 'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
05815	                 'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
05816	                 'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
05817	                 'nonce': self.nonce, 'sequence': self._sequence,
05818	                 'state': state, 'detail': detail}
05819	        raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
05820	                         separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
05821	        if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
05822	            frame['detail'] = {'truncated': True}
05823	            raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
05824	                             separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
05825	        # The deadline of the round is already bound to the transport, so
05826	        # the write of the frame is bounded by it.  The transport is
05827	        # never called with an argument that it does not know.
05828	        self.transport.write(raw + b'\n')
05829	
05830	    # -- the supervised startup --------------------------------------------
05831	    def _guarded_startup(self, action, stage):
05832	        """One startup step, guarded by the shared deadline.
05833	
05834	        The shared budget is checked **before** the step and **after**
05835	        it returned.  A step that starts after the deadline of the round
05836	        arrived never runs at all, and a step that finished after the
05837	        deadline may only clean up: it may never continue the startup of
05838	        the round and it may never write a successful reply.
05839	
05840	        The supervision publishes the cancel of the round when the
05841	        deadline arrived: the core and every later step of the startup
05842	        see it and they stop.
05843	        """
05844	        def guarded():
05845	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
05846	            action()
05847	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
05848	            return None
05849	        return guarded
05850	
05851	    def _check_startup_budget(self, stage):
05852	        """Check the shared budget of the startup of the round."""
05853	        try:
05854	            self.core.check_startup_budget(stage)
05855	        except BrokerError:
05856	            # The supervision publishes the cancel of the round: the
05857	            # core and every later step of the startup see it.
05858	            self.context.request_cancel()
05859	            self._abort_started_round()
05860	            raise
05861	
05862	    def _abort_started_round(self):
05863	        """Release what the round acquired, bounded by the shared budget."""
05864	        try:
05865	            self.core.stop('STARTUP_AFTER_DEADLINE', force_unknown=True)
05866	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05867	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'startup_abort',
05868	                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
05869	
05870	    def _run_startup(self, stage, action):
05871	        """Run one startup step under the absolute deadline of the round.
05872	
05873	        **Every** exit of this method waits for the **real** exit of the
05874	        thread of the step: the success, the timeout, the ordinary
05875	        failure, the cancellation and the ``SystemExit``.  The attempt
05876	        of the step and the thread that performs it are two different
05877	        facts and the owner of the step is released only after the real
05878	        exit of its thread.
05879	
05880	        A thread that did not exit keeps its owner: it stays in the
05881	        owners that are in flight and the round keeps the fact.  It is
05882	        never released because the attempt ended and it is never given a
05883	        new budget.
05884	        """
05885	        owner = _StartupOwner(name=stage, clock=self._clock,
05886	                              deadline=self._deadline_mono)
05887	        self._startup_owner = owner
05888	        self.context.register_inflight(owner)
05889	        try:
05890	            # The start of the step is **inside** the ownership of the
05891	            # owner and inside the ``finally`` below: a start that did
05892	            # not return is coordinated like every other exit and the
05893	            # owner is never left in the flight without a decision.
05894	            owner.start(self._guarded_startup(action, stage))
05895	            value = owner.await_result()
05896	        except BrokerError as error:
05897	            if error.code not in ('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED',
05898	                                  'BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED'):
05899	                # An ordinary failure of the step: the thread of the
05900	                # step is still coordinated in the finally below and the
05901	                # original failure is the one that leaves this method.
05902	                raise
05903	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage, 'code': error.code})
05904	            # The step did not finish before the deadline.  The owner
05905	            # keeps its thread: a late result may only clean up.
05906	            raise
05907	        finally:
05908	            # The budget of the settle is **recomputed after the await**
05909	            # and immediately before the join: a deadline that was
05910	            # published while the step was running still shortens the
05911	            # wait and a settle budget that was copied before the await
05912	            # is never used.  There is no positive floor beyond the
05913	            # time that is really left.
05914	            settle_budget = self._startup_settle_seconds()
05915	            # The real exit of the thread is asked on **every** exit.
05916	            try:
05917	                settled = owner.settle(settle_budget)
05918	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
05919	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
05920	                                           'error_type': type(error).__name__})
05921	                settled = False
05922	            if not settled:
05923	                self._startup_in_flight = owner
05924	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
05925	                                           'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
05926	            else:
05927	                self.context.release_inflight(owner)
05928	        return value
05929	
05930	    def _startup_settle_seconds(self):
05931	        """The bounded wait for the real exit of a startup thread.
05932	
05933	        The wait is bounded by the **remaining** of the absolute
05934	        deadline of the round and by the settle bound of the package.
05935	        It never publishes the cleanup deadline of the round - the
05936	        cleanup of a round that did not fail keeps its whole budget -
05937	        and it never opens a budget of its own.
05938	
05939	        The remaining is recomputed on every call and it is never
05940	        rounded **up**: a settle budget that is larger than the time
05941	        that is really left would wait past the deadline of the round.
05942	        The published cleanup deadline of the round shortens it when the
05943	        round already entered its cleanup.
05944	        """
05945	        remaining = self.context.remaining_absolute()
05946	        cleanup = self.context.cleanup_deadline_value()
05947	        if cleanup is not None:
05948	            left = max(0.0, cleanup - self._clock())
05949	            remaining = left if remaining is None else min(remaining, left)
05950	        if remaining is None:
05951	            return STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS
05952	        return max(0.0, min(remaining, STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS))
05953	
05954	    # -- the run -----------------------------------------------------------
05955	    def run(self):
05956	        """Run the round and always release what the round owns.
05957	
05958	        The loop has a ``finally`` that does not depend on the diagnosis
05959	        of the round and not on the success of a write: a round that
05960	        could not write its first reply still releases the job and the
05961	        handles that it created.
05962	        """
05963	        self._fix_deadline()
05964	        # The core and the loop read the same clock: the deadlines of
05965	        # the round and the ONE cleanup deadline are in one domain.
05966	        binder_clock = getattr(self.core, 'bind_clock', None)
05967	        if callable(binder_clock):
05968	            binder_clock(self._clock)
05969	        self.core.bind_absolute_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
05970	        binder = getattr(self.transport, 'bind_deadline', None)
05971	        if callable(binder):
05972	            # The transport of the channel is bound to the deadline of
05973	            # the round before the job of the round exists: a read or a
05974	            # write of the channel can never outlive the round.
05975	            binder(self._deadline_mono)
05976	        try:
05977	            try:
05978	                self._run_startup('ARM', self.core.arm)
05979	            except BrokerError as error:
05980	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
05981	                            terminal=True)
05982	                self._write_receipt('ARM_FAILED')
05983	                return 3
05984	            self._reply('READY', self.core.state, {'broker': self.core.broker_identity})
05985	            try:
05986	                self._run_startup('LAUNCH', self.core.launch)
05987	            except BrokerError as error:
05988	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
05989	                            terminal=True)
05990	                self._write_receipt('LAUNCH_FAILED')
05991	                return 3
05992	            self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
05993	            exit_code = self._loop()
05994	            self._write_receipt(self.core.stop_reason)
05995	            return exit_code
05996	        finally:
05997	            self._ensure_stopped()
05998	
05999	    def _ensure_stopped(self):
06000	        """Release the resources of the round, whatever happened before.
06001	
06002	        The release never raises: a failure of the release is kept by the
06003	        core and it never replaces the first failure of the round.
06004	        """
06005	        if self._stopped:
06006	            return
06007	        self._stopped = True
06008	        if self._startup_in_flight is not None:
06009	            # A startup step is still running: the core belongs to the
06010	            # in-flight owner and the round keeps the fact.  The cleanup
06011	            # of the core would race with the step that is still inside
06012	            # the operating system, so it is not started here.
06013	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
06014	                                       'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
06015	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06016	            return
06017	        try:
06018	            self.core.stop(self.core.stop_reason or 'LOOP_EXIT', force_unknown=True)
06019	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06020	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
06021	                                        'error_type': type(error).__name__})
06022	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06023	
06024	    def _loop(self):
06025	        self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
06026	        while True:
06027	            now = self._clock()
06028	            if now >= self._deadline_mono:
06029	                return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
06030	            if self.core.overflowed():
06031	                return self._stop('BOUND_EXCEEDED', 4)
06032	            timeout = min(POLL_SECONDS, max(0.01, self._deadline_mono - now))
06033	            try:
06034	                raw = self.transport.read(timeout)
06035	            except BrokerError:
06036	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_ERROR', 4)
06037	            if raw is None:
06038	                # A missing heartbeat is a secondary signal.  The absolute
06039	                # deadline stays the authority of the round.
06040	                if self._wall() - self._last_frame_wall > HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:
06041	                    return self._stop('HEARTBEAT_MISSING', 4)
06042	                continue
06043	            if raw == b'':
06044	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_EOF', 4)
06045	            # The hard cap of the channel is checked before the frame is
06046	            # decoded: a frame that does not fit at all is refused
06047	            # without being accepted.
06048	            if self._frames + self._sent_frames + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
06049	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
06050	            try:
06051	                frame = decode_command(raw, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
06052	                                       candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
06053	                                       authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
06054	                                       nonce=self.nonce, last_sequence=self._last_sequence)
06055	            except CustodyError as error:
06056	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'channel', 'code': error.code})
06057	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_REFUSED', 4)
06058	            # The reservation of the terminal exchange is **consumed** by
06059	            # the terminal frame itself.  A STOP that arrives near the
06060	            # limit of the budget is a legal frame: it is never refused
06061	            # because of the reservation that was kept for it.  A frame
06062	            # that is not terminal never borrows the reservation and it
06063	            # fails closed when the total does not fit.
06064	            terminal = frame['command'] == 'STOP'
06065	            try:
06066	                self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
06067	            except BrokerError:
06068	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
06069	            self._frames += 1
06070	            if self._frames > self._max_frames:
06071	                return self._stop('FRAME_COUNT_EXCEEDED', 4)
06072	            # Only a frame that was decoded and that carries the identity
06073	            # of the round refreshes the heartbeat of the round.  A frame
06074	            # that was refused never does.
06075	            self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
06076	            self._last_alive_wall = self._last_frame_wall
06077	            self._last_sequence = frame['sequence']
06078	            if frame['command'] == 'STATUS':
06079	                self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
06080	                continue
06081	            return self._stop('STOP_COMMAND', 0)
06082	
06083	    def _stop(self, reason, exit_code):
06084	        unknown = reason != 'STOP_COMMAND'
06085	        self.core.stop(reason, force_unknown=unknown)
06086	        self._reply('STOPPED', self.core.state, self.core.summary(), terminal=True)
06087	        return exit_code
06088	
06089	    def _write_receipt(self, reason):
06090	        """Write the terminal receipt of the round into the run root.
06091	
06092	        The receipt is the evidence that survives a parent that died.  It
06093	        is written once, it is bounded and it is never replaced by a
06094	        successful claim.  A failure of the write never replaces the
06095	        first failure of the round.
06096	        """
06097	        evidence = self.core.evidence()
06098	        evidence['reason'] = reason
06099	        evidence['channel'] = {'frames_received': self._frames,
06100	                               'last_sequence_received': self._last_sequence,
06101	                               'frame_limit': self._max_frames}
06102	        evidence['deadline_wall'] = self._deadline_wall
06103	        evidence['deadline_extended'] = False
06104	        raw = json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
06105	                         indent=2).encode('utf-8')
06106	        if len(raw) > MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES:
06107	            raw = json.dumps({'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-receipt/1',
06108	                              'reason': reason, 'truncated': True,
06109	                              'stage_pass_claim': False}).encode('utf-8')
06110	        path = Path(self.core.run_root) / 'temp' / 'native' / 'custody-terminal.json'
06111	        try:
06112	            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
06113	            path.write_bytes(raw)
06114	        except OSError:
06115	            return False
06116	        return True
06117	
06118	
06119	# -- the fixed entry of the broker -----------------------------------------
06120
