06121	def build_broker_command(*, plan_path, plan_sha256, run_root, nonce, python=None):
06122	    """The fixed command of the broker.
06123	
06124	    The command starts exactly this broker with one sealed plan document.
06125	    There is no dynamic import of a caller supplied module and no arbitrary
06126	    argument vector: the only arguments are the plan, its digest, the run
06127	    root of the round and the nonce of the session.
06128	    """
06129	    script = Path(__file__).resolve()
06130	    if not script.is_file():
06131	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_SCRIPT_MISSING')
06132	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(plan_sha256)):
06133	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_INVALID')
06134	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
06135	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
06136	    executable = python or sys.executable
06137	    return [executable, '-I', '-B', str(script),
06138	            '--plan', str(plan_path), '--plan-sha256', str(plan_sha256),
06139	            '--run-root', str(run_root), '--nonce', str(nonce)]
06140	
06141	
06142	class BrokerProcess:
06143	    """The original handle of the broker process of one round.
06144	
06145	    The parent keeps the process handle, the pipes of the private channel
06146	    and the identity of the executable.  The replies of the broker are
06147	    read through one bounded background reader, so the parent never blocks
06148	    on the channel and the deadline of the round is never held up by a
06149	    silent broker.
06150	
06151	    The handle is **deadline aware**: it says so with its own marker and
06152	    it takes the absolute deadline of every write.  A handle that is not
06153	    deadline aware is never assumed to be deadline aware by the parent.
06154	    """
06155	
06156	    #: The marker of a handle that understands an absolute deadline.
06157	    channel_deadline_aware = True
06158	
06159	    def __init__(self, process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
06160	                 clock=None, context=None):
06161	        self._process = process
06162	        self._channel = channel
06163	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
06164	        # The ONE context of the launch.  The close of the broker reads
06165	        # the absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of
06166	        # the round from it and it never opens a budget of its own.
06167	        self._context = context
06168	        self.pid = process.pid
06169	        self.executable = executable
06170	        self.executable_sha256 = executable_sha256
06171	        # Whether the reader of the channel really stopped when the channel
06172	        # was closed.  It is ``None`` until the close happened and it stays
06173	        # ``False`` when the reader was still alive after the bounded join.
06174	        self.reader_finished = None
06175	        # The absolute deadline of the round, handed over by the parent.
06176	        # The parent may only shorten it.
06177	        self.write_deadline = None
06178	        self.close_facts = None
06179	
06180	    def bind_channel_deadline(self, deadline):
06181	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the private channel.
06182	
06183	        The deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock
06184	        and it may only shorten the deadline that is already bound: a
06185	        later deadline is refused.
06186	        """
06187	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06188	        if value is None:
06189	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID')
06190	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
06191	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
06192	        self.write_deadline = value
06193	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
06194	        if callable(binder):
06195	            binder(value)
06196	        return value
06197	
06198	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
06199	        """Write one frame of the parent, bounded by the deadline.
06200	
06201	        The deadline is explicit and it is checked: a write that is not
06202	        bounded by an absolute deadline would let a broker that never
06203	        reads hold the supervisor of the round forever.
06204	        """
06205	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06206	        if value is None:
06207	            value = self.write_deadline
06208	        if value is None:
06209	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_MISSING')
06210	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
06211	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
06212	        return self._channel.write(raw, value)
06213	
06214	    def read(self, timeout):
06215	        return self._channel.read(timeout)
06216	
06217	    def close(self, deadline=None):
06218	        """Close the original handle of the broker.
06219	
06220	        The close happens even when the exchange of the stop failed: the
06221	        resource of this round is released either way.
06222	
06223	        The facts of the close are returned and they are named: a handle
06224	        that was not closed, a reader that did not stop and a process that
06225	        did not exit are facts of the round and they are never hidden
06226	        behind a success.  The release of the original process handle is
06227	        explicit and it is never left to the garbage collector.
06228	
06229	        The waits of the close are bounded by **one** absolute deadline:
06230	        the deadline that the parent bound before it closed the handle.
06231	        The remaining time is recomputed before every wait, so that a
06232	        wait that already consumed part of the budget never gets the
06233	        whole budget again and no layer of the cleanup adds a budget of
06234	        its own.
06235	
06236	        The close may be **repeated** inside the same deadline: an item
06237	        that was confirmed is never closed a second time and an item
06238	        that was not confirmed is coordinated again.  The attempt that
06239	        ended is not a release: only the facts of the close prove it.
06240	
06241	        The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
06242	        round and not a fact of the last attempt: a repeated close keeps
06243	        the first error that the reader really had, it publishes the
06244	        source of that error and it never turns a real error into a
06245	        ``None``.  The resource facts of the close may converge while
06246	        the error history of the reader stays: a close that returned is
06247	        not a release and a retry is not an amnesty.
06248	
06249	        The **endpoint is the only owner** of the files of the channel.
06250	        The broker process therefore never closes the standard input and
06251	        the standard output of the process by itself: it closes the
06252	        channel, and the channel closes the files that it owns.
06253	        """
06254	        value = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
06255	        budget = max(0.0, value - self._clock())
06256	        facts = {'channel_closed': False,
06257	                 'channel_reader_finished': None,
06258	                 'channel_reader_error': None,
06259	                 'channel_reader_error_source': None,
06260	                 'channel_reader_secondary_errors': [],
06261	                 'channel_overflow': False,
06262	                 'channel_overflow_facts': {},
06263	                 'close_cancellation': None,
06264	                 'channel_retained_workers': 0,
06265	                 'write_end_closed': False,
06266	                 'read_end_closed': False,
06267	                 'stdin_closed': False,
06268	                 'stdout_closed': False,
06269	                 'process_exited': False,
06270	                 'exit_code': None,
06271	                 'terminated': False,
06272	                 'process_handle_released': False,
06273	                 'process_handle_release_method': None,
06274	                 'worker_thread_handle_released': False,
06275	                 'cleanup_budget_seconds': budget,
06276	                 'cleanup_deadline': value}
06277	        # The facts of a previous attempt are kept: an item that was
06278	        # confirmed is never closed a second time and its fact stays
06279	        # true.
06280	        previous = self.close_facts if isinstance(self.close_facts, dict) else {}
06281	        for key, confirmed in previous.items():
06282	            if key in ('cleanup_budget_seconds', 'cleanup_deadline'):
06283	                continue
06284	            if (confirmed is True
06285	                    or (key == 'exit_code' and confirmed is not None)
06286	                    or key == 'process_handle_release_method'
06287	                    or key == 'close_cancellation'):
06288	                facts[key] = confirmed
06289	        # The channel and its endpoints use the very same absolute
06290	        # deadline: they never start a wait of their own.
06291	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
06292	        if callable(binder):
06293	            try:
06294	                binder(value)
06295	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06296	                pass
06297	        if not facts['channel_closed']:
06298	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
06299	        elif not self._channel_complete(facts):
06300	            # A previous attempt already called the close of the
06301	            # channel, but it did not confirm **every** item of it: the
06302	            # read end, the worker of an endpoint and the original
06303	            # handle of its thread may still be open.  The items that
06304	            # were not confirmed are coordinated again, inside the very
06305	            # same deadline.  A close that returned is not a release.
06306	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
06307	        else:
06308	            channel_facts = None
06309	        if channel_facts is not None:
06310	            facts.update(channel_facts)
06311	        # The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
06312	        # round: a repeated close never turns a real error of the reader
06313	        # into a ``None``.  The first error is the error of the round and
06314	        # a later error is kept beside it as a secondary one.
06315	        self._keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous)
06316	        # The files of the channel belong to the endpoints of the
06317	        # channel.  The fact of their close is the fact of the channel.
06318	        facts['stdin_closed'] = facts['write_end_closed']
06319	        facts['stdout_closed'] = facts['read_end_closed']
06320	        if not facts['process_exited']:
06321	            try:
06322	                self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, value - self._clock()))
06323	                facts['process_exited'] = True
06324	                facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
06325	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06326	                # Only the broker that this parent holds is terminated, and
06327	                # its exit is confirmed afterwards with a bounded wait.
06328	                try:
06329	                    self._process.terminate()
06330	                    facts['terminated'] = True
06331	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06332	                    pass
06333	                try:
06334	                    self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, value - self._clock()))
06335	                    facts['process_exited'] = True
06336	                    facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
06337	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06338	                    pass
06339	        if not facts['process_handle_released']:
06340	            facts.update(self._release_process_handle(facts['process_exited']))
06341	        self.close_facts = facts
06342	        self.reader_finished = facts['channel_reader_finished']
06343	        return facts
06344	
06345	    @staticmethod
06346	    def _keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous):
06347	        """Keep the history of the error of the reader of the channel.
06348	
06349	        The **first** error that the reader of the channel really had is
06350	        the error of the round: every later close keeps it and it is
06351	        never turned into a ``None`` by a repeated close.  The resource
06352	        facts of the close may converge, but a real error of the reader
06353	        is never lost by the retry: the copy of the facts of a previous
06354	        attempt keeps the facts that are ``True`` and it does **not**
06355	        keep a code, so the error is restored here from the history
06356	        itself.
06357	
06358	        A later error is a **secondary** error: it is kept beside the
06359	        first one and it never replaces it.  The source of the error is
06360	        published with it: ``FIRST_CLOSE`` when the close that is running
06361	        now is the one that really observed the error and
06362	        ``REPEATED_CLOSE`` when this attempt carries the error of an
06363	        earlier one.  The fact of the error itself is the history and it
06364	        is never replaced by a boolean.
06365	
06366	        The named fact of the cancellation of the close is independent
06367	        from this history and it is never merged with it.
06368	        """
06369	        previous = previous if isinstance(previous, dict) else {}
06370	        first = previous.get('channel_reader_error')
06371	        current = facts.get('channel_reader_error')
06372	        secondary = list(previous.get('channel_reader_secondary_errors') or [])
06373	        if first:
06374	            if current and current != first:
06375	                # A second error of the reader is kept beside the first
06376	                # one: the first error stays the error of the round.
06377	                if current not in secondary:
06378	                    secondary.append(current)
06379	            facts['channel_reader_secondary_errors'] = secondary
06380	            facts['channel_reader_error'] = first
06381	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
06382	            return facts
06383	        if current:
06384	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'FIRST_CLOSE'
06385	        return facts
06386	
06387	    @staticmethod
06388	    def _channel_complete(facts):
06389	        """Whether the close of the channel confirmed every item of it.
06390	
06391	        The items are the two ends of the channel, the reader of the
06392	        channel, the workers of the endpoints and the original handles
06393	        of their threads.  A fact that is missing is a missing fact and
06394	        not a success.
06395	        """
06396	        if facts.get('channel_reader_finished') is not True:
06397	            return False
06398	        if facts.get('read_end_closed') is not True:
06399	            return False
06400	        if facts.get('write_end_closed') is not True:
06401	            return False
06402	        if int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) != 0:
06403	            return False
06404	        if facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released') is not True:
06405	            return False
06406	        return True
06407	
06408	    def _close_channel(self, facts):
06409	        """Close the channel of the round and report its facts.
06410	
06411	        The method returns the facts of the close of the channel and it
06412	        never raises: the failure of the close of the channel is a fact
06413	        of the round and it is named in the facts.
06414	        """
06415	        try:
06416	            channel_facts = self._channel.close()
06417	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06418	            facts['channel_closed'] = False
06419	            return None
06420	        facts['channel_closed'] = True
06421	        facts['channel_reader_finished'] = getattr(self._channel,
06422	                                                    'reader_finished', None)
06423	        if not isinstance(channel_facts, dict):
06424	            return None
06425	        facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('write_end_closed'))
06426	        facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('read_end_closed'))
06427	        facts['channel_reader_error'] = channel_facts.get('reader_error')
06428	        # The overflow of the channel is a **named** failure of the
06429	        # round and it travels with the facts of the close: a channel
06430	        # that overflowed is never a channel that ended normally.
06431	        facts['channel_overflow'] = bool(channel_facts.get('overflow'))
06432	        facts['channel_overflow_facts'] = dict(
06433	            channel_facts.get('overflow_facts') or {})
06434	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = int(
06435	            (channel_facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
06436	            + (channel_facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
06437	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
06438	            channel_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
06439	        return channel_facts
06440	
06441	    def _cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
06442	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of the broker.
06443	
06444	        The deadline is the one that the parent bound before it closed
06445	        the handle.  It may only be shortened, never extended, and every
06446	        wait of the cleanup recomputes the time that is left.
06447	
06448	        The **context** of the launch is read as well: a cleanup that
06449	        runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
06450	        deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
06451	        """
06452	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06453	        if value is None:
06454	            value = self.write_deadline
06455	        context = self._context
06456	        if context is not None:
06457	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
06458	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
06459	                if candidate is None:
06460	                    continue
06461	                if value is None or candidate < value:
06462	                    value = candidate
06463	        if value is None:
06464	            value = self._clock() + CLEANUP_BUDGET_SECONDS
06465	        elif self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
06466	            value = self.write_deadline
06467	        return value
06468	
06469	    def _release_process_handle(self, exited=False):
06470	        """Release the original process handle, explicitly and by platform.
06471	
06472	        The release is recorded and it is never left to the garbage
06473	        collector: on Windows the original handle of the process is closed
06474	        through its own handle object and on POSIX the child process has
06475	        no separate handle that the parent owns.
06476	
06477	        The original handle of the process is closed **only** when the
06478	        exit of the process was really confirmed.  A process whose exit
06479	        was not confirmed may still be alive and its original handle is
06480	        the only handle that the parent owns for it: the handle stays
06481	        with its owner and a later close may release it.  A close that
06482	        succeeds is never a proof that the process ended.
06483	        """
06484	        if not exited:
06485	            return {'process_handle_released': False,
06486	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED'}
06487	        if os.name == 'nt':
06488	            handle = getattr(self._process, '_handle', None)
06489	            close = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
06490	            if close is None:
06491	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
06492	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_NO_EXPLICIT_HANDLE'}
06493	            try:
06494	                close()
06495	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06496	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
06497	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED'}
06498	            return {'process_handle_released': True,
06499	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE'}
06500	        return {'process_handle_released': True,
06501	                'process_handle_release_method': 'POSIX_PROCESS_EXIT'}
06502	
06503	
06504	class _LaunchAggregate:
06505	    """The thin persistent owner of **one** launch.
06506	
06507	    Every launch of this package has exactly one aggregate and it is
06508	    created **before** the first side effect of the launch.  It holds
06509	    the original references of the launch: the process, the channel,
06510	    the partial endpoints, the workers of the endpoints and the context
06511	    of the launch.
06512	
06513	    The aggregate is the entry that the outer owner of a failed launch
06514	    uses.  A construction that did not return, a channel whose reader
06515	    thread did not start and a channel that could not be handed over
06516	    are found **here** and never through a local variable that is
06517	    ``None`` at that moment.
06518	
06519	    The release of the aggregate is ordered by the **dependency**: the
06520	    channel owns the files of the process and the worker of an endpoint
06521	    owns the handle of its thread, so the channel is coordinated first
06522	    and the process is coordinated after it.  A process whose channel
06523	    took the files is never closed by the process helper: a file that
06524	    is closed under a live worker is a file that a worker reads from.
06525	    """
06526	
06527	    def __init__(self, *, launch_id=None, custody=None, clock=None,
06528	                 context=None):
06529	        self.launch_id = launch_id or 'launch'
06530	        self.custody = custody
06531	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
06532	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
06533	        self.process = None
06534	        self.channel = None
06535	        self.endpoints = []
06536	        self.workers = []
06537	        self.context = context
06538	        self.facts = {}
06539	        # Whether the exit of the process of this launch was already
06540	        # **confirmed**.  A retry of the same owner coordinates only the
06541	        # parts that were not confirmed yet: it never terminates and
06542	        # never waits a second time.
06543	        self.process_exit_confirmed = False
06544	
06545	    # -- the bindings of the launch ---------------------------------------
06546	    def bind_process(self, process):
06547	        """Bind the original process of this launch."""
06548	        with self._lock:
06549	            self.process = process
06550	        return process
06551	
06552	    def bind_channel(self, channel):
06553	        """Bind the channel of this launch."""
06554	        with self._lock:
06555	            if channel is not None:
06556	                self.channel = channel
06557	        return channel
06558	
06559	    def bind_endpoint(self, endpoint):
06560	        """Bind one endpoint of this launch."""
06561	        with self._lock:
06562	            self.endpoints.append(endpoint)
06563	        return endpoint
06564	
06565	    def bind_worker(self, worker):
06566	        """Bind one worker of this launch."""
06567	        with self._lock:
06568	            self.workers.append(worker)
06569	        return worker
06570	
06571	    def bind_context(self, context):
06572	        """Bind the ONE context of this launch."""
06573	        with self._lock:
06574	            if context is not None:
06575	                self.context = context
06576	        return self.context
06577	
06578	    # -- the custody protocol of the launcher -----------------------------
06579	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None):
06580	        """Take one original resource of this launch.
06581	
06582	        The protocol is the protocol of the table of the launcher: the
06583	        channel of a launch registers itself **before** it builds
06584	        anything, so that a partial channel is always reachable.
06585	        """
06586	        with self._lock:
06587	            if kind == 'channel' and resource is not None:
06588	                self.channel = resource
06589	            elif kind == 'endpoint':
06590	                self.endpoints.append(resource)
06591	            elif kind == 'worker':
06592	                self.workers.append(resource)
06593	        return resource
06594	
06595	    def forget(self, resource):
06596	        """Leave the launch: the resource was handed over or released."""
06597	        with self._lock:
06598	            if self.channel is resource:
06599	                self.channel = None
06600	            self.endpoints = [item for item in self.endpoints
06601	                              if item is not resource]
06602	            self.workers = [item for item in self.workers
06603	                            if item is not resource]
06604	        return None
06605	
06606	    def items(self):
06607	        """The original resources that this launch still owns."""
06608	        with self._lock:
06609	            return {'process': self.process, 'channel': self.channel,
06610	                    'endpoints': list(self.endpoints),
06611	                    'workers': list(self.workers)}
06612	
06613	    # -- the coordinated release ------------------------------------------
06614	    def _release_deadline(self, deadline=None):
06615	        """The absolute deadline that the release of this launch uses.
06616	
06617	        The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
06618	        context of this launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
06619	        context is read **again** at the moment of the release and it is
06620	        never copied into a number of the launch: a layer that copied
06621	        the number of a deadline would have a deadline of its own.
06622	        """
06623	        try:
06624	            value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06625	        except BrokerError:
06626	            value = None
06627	        context = self.context
06628	        if context is not None:
06629	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
06630	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
06631	                if candidate is None:
06632	                    continue
06633	                if value is None or candidate < value:
06634	                    value = candidate
06635	        return value
06636	
06637	    def release(self, deadline=None):
06638	        """Coordinate the resources of this launch, in the dependency order.
06639	
06640	        The channel is coordinated **first** and the process is
06641	        coordinated after it.  A close that returned is not a release:
06642	        the channel is released only when **every** fact of it is
06643	        closed - the reader, the two ends, the workers and the original
06644	        handles of their threads.  An incomplete close keeps the
06645	        channel with its owner and it is named.
06646	
06647	        The original handle of the process is released only after the
06648	        exit of the process was really confirmed.
06649	
06650	        The release never raises before every safe step was executed:
06651	        the original cancellation of the launch is raised at the end and
06652	        the ordinary failures are named beside it.
06653	        """
06654	        released = []
06655	        retained = []
06656	        failures = {}
06657	        cancellations = []
06658	        channel = self.channel
06659	        if channel is not None:
06660	            facts = {}
06661	            try:
06662	                facts = channel.close()
06663	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
06664	                cancellations.append(error)
06665	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06666	                failures['channel'] = type(error).__name__
06667	            if not isinstance(facts, dict):
06668	                facts = {}
06669	            complete = (bool(facts.get('reader_finished'))
06670	                        and bool(facts.get('read_end_closed'))
06671	                        and bool(facts.get('write_end_closed'))
06672	                        and int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) == 0
06673	                        and bool(facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released')))
06674	            if complete:
06675	                released.append('channel')
06676	                self.forget(channel)
06677	                if self.custody is not None:
06678	                    self.custody.forget(channel)
06679	            else:
06680	                # A close that returned is not a release: the channel
06681	                # keeps its owner and its original reference.
06682	                failures['channel'] = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE'
06683	                retained.append('channel')
06684	                if self.custody is not None:
06685	                    self.custody.retain('channel', channel,
06686	                                         'release_incomplete', launch=self)
06687	        process = self.process
06688	        if process is not None:
06689	            # The files of the process belong to the channel when the
06690	            # channel took them: the process helper never closes them.
06691	            close_pipes = channel is None
06692	            outcome = {'failures': {}, 'exited': False, 'retained_streams': []}
06693	            try:
06694	                # The deadline of the launch is read **again** after the
06695	                # channel was coordinated: a cleanup deadline that was
06696	                # published in the meantime shortens the wait of the
06697	                # process and it is never a number that was copied
06698	                # once.
06699	                outcome = _release_launch_process(
06700	                    process, deadline=self._release_deadline(deadline),
06701	                    close_pipes=close_pipes,
06702	                    exited=self.process_exit_confirmed,
06703	                    context=self.context)
06704	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
06705	                cancellations.append(error)
06706	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06707	                outcome['failures']['process'] = type(error).__name__
06708	            failures.update(outcome['failures'])
06709	            # A raw stream that could not be closed stays a reachable
06710	            # owned resource: it keeps its **original** reference and it
06711	            # is never replaced by a summary of the failure.
06712	            for name, stream in outcome.get('retained_streams') or []:
06713	                retained.append(name)
06714	                if self.custody is not None:
06715	                    self.custody.retain(name, stream, 'close_failed',
06716	                                        launch=self)
06717	            if outcome['exited']:
06718	                self.process_exit_confirmed = True
06719	            # The original process is forgotten **only** when its exit
06720	            # was confirmed and the original handle of the process was
06721	            # released.  A close of the handle that failed keeps the
06722	            # process with its owner and the same owner may retry it.
06723	            handle_released = 'handle' not in outcome['failures']
06724	            if outcome['exited'] and handle_released:
06725	                released.append('process')
06726	                with self._lock:
06727	                    self.process = None
06728	                if self.custody is not None:
06729	                    self.custody.forget(process)
06730	            else:
06731	                retained.append('process')
06732	                if self.custody is not None:
06733	                    self.custody.retain('process', process, 'release_failed',
06734	                                         launch=self)
06735	        self.facts = {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained),
06736	                      'failures': dict(failures)}
06737	        if cancellations:
06738	            # The original cancellation is raised after every safe step
06739	            # of the release was executed and the ordinary failures stay
06740	            # named next to it.
06741	            raise cancellations[0]
06742	        return self.facts
06743	
06744	
06745	class _LauncherCustody:
06746	    """The persistent owner of the original resources of a failed launch.
06747	
06748	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
06749	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
06750	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
06751	    nobody owns.
06752	
06753	    Every original resource of such a launch enters this table **with
06754	    its original reference** and it stays there until a confirmed
06755	    release: the round never loses the ownership of a resource that it
06756	    created, and a pid is never used to find a process again.
06757	
06758	    The table is the recovery entry point of the launcher.  It is
06759	    explicit and it is finite: a caller that wants the resources of a
06760	    failed launch released asks for it.
06761	
06762	    The resources of a launch are coordinated by the **aggregate** of
06763	    that launch and not by the order of this table: the channel of a
06764	    launch is closed before the process of that launch, because the
06765	    channel owns the files of the process.  A resource that belongs to
06766	    a launch is never released a second time by this table.
06767	    """
06768	
06769	    def __init__(self):
06770	        self._items = []
06771	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
06772	        # The ONE context of the round, when the table is bound to it.
06773	        # A recovery that runs without a deadline of its own reads the
06774	        # absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of the
06775	        # round from it and it never opens a fresh budget.
06776	        self._context = None
06777	
06778	    def bind_context(self, context):
06779	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to this table."""
06780	        if context is not None:
06781	            self._context = context
06782	        return self._context
06783	
06784	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
06785	        """The ONE deadline that a release of this table really uses."""
06786	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06787	        context = self._context
06788	        if context is not None:
06789	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
06790	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
06791	                if candidate is None:
06792	                    continue
06793	                if value is None or candidate < value:
06794	                    value = candidate
06795	        return value
06796	
06797	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None, launch=None):
06798	        """Take one original resource into the table of the launcher.
06799	
06800	        The table never names the same resource twice: a resource that
06801	        is already with its owner is not entered a second time and the
06802	        table converges.
06803	
06804	        The ``launch`` is the aggregate that owns the resource.  A
06805	        resource that belongs to a launch is coordinated by that
06806	        launch and it is never released a second time by this table.
06807	        """
06808	        with self._lock:
06809	            for item in self._items:
06810	                if item['kind'] == kind and item['resource'] is resource:
06811	                    return resource
06812	            self._items.append({'kind': kind, 'resource': resource,
06813	                                'detail': detail, 'launch': launch})
06814	        return resource
06815	
06816	    def forget(self, resource):
06817	        """Leave the table: the resource was handed over to its owner."""
06818	        with self._lock:
06819	            remaining = [item for item in self._items
06820	                         if item['resource'] is not resource]
06821	            self._items = remaining
06822	
06823	    def items(self):
06824	        """The original resources that the launcher still owns."""
06825	        with self._lock:
06826	            return list(self._items)
06827	
06828	    def release(self, deadline=None, context=None):
06829	        """Try to release every original resource of the table.
06830	
06831	        The release of every resource is independent of the others: a
06832	        resource that could not be released stays in the table with its
06833	        original reference and it is named in the report.
06834	
06835	        The **launches** are coordinated first: every aggregate closes
06836	        the channel of its launch before the process of that launch.
06837	        The resources that belong to no launch are released afterwards
06838	        and a resource of a launch is never released twice.
06839	
06840	        The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline.
06841	        A recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a
06842	        fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
06843	        """
06844	        released = []
06845	        retained = []
06846	        cancellations = []
06847	        with self._lock:
06848	            items = list(self._items)
06849	        keep = []
06850	        for item in items:
06851	            if item['kind'] == 'aggregate':
06852	                launch = item['resource']
06853	                try:
06854	                    # The deadline of the table is read **again** for
06855	                    # every launch: a cleanup deadline that was
06856	                    # published in the meantime shortens the release of
06857	                    # the launch.
06858	                    report = launch.release(
06859	                        deadline=self._effective_deadline(deadline))
06860	                except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
06861	                    cancellations.append(error)
06862	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
06863	                except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06864	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
06865	                released.extend(report.get('released') or [])
06866	                names = list(report.get('retained') or [])
06867	                retained.extend(names)
06868	                if names:
06869	                    keep.append(item)
06870	                continue
06871	            if item.get('launch') is not None:
06872	                # The launch of this resource coordinates it: the table
06873	                # never releases it a second time.
06874	                keep.append(item)
06875	                retained.append(item['kind'])
06876	                continue
06877	            resource = item['resource']
06878	            try:
06879	                if item['kind'] == 'process':
06880	                    outcome = _release_launch_process(
06881	                        resource, deadline=self._effective_deadline(deadline),
06882	                        close_pipes=True, context=self._context)
06883	                    if outcome['failures']:
06884	                        keep.append(item)
06885	                        retained.append(item['kind'])
06886	                        # A raw stream that could not be closed stays a
06887	                        # reachable owned resource of the launcher.
06888	                        for name, stream in outcome.get('retained_streams') or []:
06889	                            self.retain(name, stream, 'close_failed')
06890	                        continue
06891	                else:
06892	                    close = getattr(resource, 'close', None)
06893	                    if callable(close):
06894	                        close()
06895	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
06896	                keep.append(item)
06897	                retained.append(item['kind'])
06898	                continue
06899	            released.append(item['kind'])
06900	        with self._lock:
06901	            # The table is rebuilt from the entries that are **still**
06902	            # in it: an entry that was forgotten while the release ran -
06903	            # a process that the aggregate of its launch released - is
06904	            # never resurrected as a detached entry.  The entries that
06905	            # were added while the release ran are new resources of the
06906	            # round and they are kept.
06907	            kept = {id(item['resource']) for item in keep}
06908	            snapshot = {id(item['resource']) for item in items}
06909	            self._items = [item for item in self._items
06910	                           if id(item['resource']) not in snapshot
06911	                           or id(item['resource']) in kept]
06912	        if cancellations:
06913	            raise cancellations[0]
06914	        return {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained)}
06915	
06916	
06917	#: The one table of the original resources of the launches of this
06918	#: package.  It is empty after a launch that was handed over and it
06919	#: names the resources of a launch that was not.
06920	_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY = _LauncherCustody()
06921	
06922	
06923	def release_retained_launches(deadline=None, *, context=None):
06924	    """The recovery entry point of the launcher of this package.
06925	
06926	    The caller asks for the original resources of the launches that were
06927	    not handed over to be released, and the report names the resources
06928	    that could not be released.
06929	
06930	    The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline of the
06931	    round: a recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never
06932	    waits a fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be
06933	    alive.
06934	
06935	    The deadline of the context is **read again** here: a recovery
06936	    that runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
06937	    deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
06938	    """
06939	    value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06940	    if context is not None:
06941	        _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
06942	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.release(deadline=value)
06943	
06944	
06945	def _launcher_custody_items():
06946	    """The original resources that the launcher of this package owns.
06947	
06948	    The list is a copy and every entry carries the **original** object:
06949	    a pid is never used to find a process of the round again.
06950	    """
06951	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.items()
06952	
06953	
06954	def _release_budget(deadline=None, context=None):
06955	    """The seconds that are left for the wait of a process of a launch.
06956	
06957	    The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
06958	    context of the launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
06959	    context is read **again** at the moment of the wait: a cleanup
06960	    deadline that was published while the channel of the launch was
06961	    closed shortens the wait of the process.  A recovery never opens a
06962	    budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of seconds for
06963	    a process that may still be alive.
06964	
06965	    The bound of the package is used only when neither the caller nor
06966	    the context of the launch brought a deadline at all.
06967	    """
06968	    try:
06969	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
06970	    except BrokerError:
06971	        # A deadline that cannot be understood is no deadline: the
06972	        # bound of the package is used and it is never a budget that
06973	        # the caller did not ask for.
06974	        value = None
06975	    if context is not None:
06976	        for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
06977	                          context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
06978	            if candidate is None:
06979	                continue
06980	            if value is None or candidate < value:
06981	                value = candidate
06982	    if value is None:
06983	        return CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
06984	    return max(0.0, value - time.monotonic())
06985	
06986	
06987	def _release_launch_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True,
06988	                            exited=None, context=None):
06989	    """Terminate one process of a launch and report the two facts.
06990	
06991	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
06992	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed when
06993	    nobody else owns them.
06994	
06995	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: the
06996	    deadline is read **again** from the context of the launch at the
06997	    moment of the wait and a recovery never opens a budget of its own.
06998	
06999	    The ``exited`` argument is the exit that an **earlier** attempt of
07000	    the same owner already confirmed: a retry coordinates only the
07001	    parts that were not confirmed yet and it never terminates and
07002	    never waits a second time.
07003	
07004	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
07005	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
07006	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
07007	    it and it is never closed under it.
07008	
07009	    The report names every step that did not succeed and it names the
07010	    **exit** of the process as a separate fact: a close that succeeded
07011	    is never a proof that the process ended.  A raw stream that could
07012	    not be closed is returned with its **original** reference: it stays
07013	    a reachable owned resource and it is never replaced by a summary.
07014	    """
07015	    failures = {}
07016	    retained_streams = []
07017	    confirmed = bool(exited)
07018	    if not confirmed:
07019	        try:
07020	            process.terminate()
07021	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07022	            failures['terminate'] = type(error).__name__
07023	        budget = _release_budget(deadline, context)
07024	        try:
07025	            process.wait(timeout=budget)
07026	            confirmed = True
07027	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07028	            failures['wait'] = type(error).__name__
07029	    if confirmed and os.name == 'nt':
07030	        # The original handle of the process is closed explicitly and it
07031	        # is never left to the garbage collector.  A handle that could
07032	        # not be closed keeps its owner.
07033	        handle = getattr(process, '_handle', None)
07034	        closer = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
07035	        if callable(closer):
07036	            try:
07037	                closer()
07038	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07039	                failures['handle'] = type(error).__name__
07040	    if close_pipes:
07041	        for name in ('stdin', 'stdout', 'stderr'):
07042	            stream = getattr(process, name, None)
07043	            if stream is None:
07044	                continue
07045	            try:
07046	                stream.close()
07047	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07048	                failures[name] = type(error).__name__
07049	                # The stream keeps its **original** reference: it stays
07050	                # a reachable owned resource of the round.
07051	                retained_streams.append((name, stream))
07052	    return {'failures': failures, 'exited': confirmed,
07053	            'retained_streams': retained_streams}
07054	
07055	
07056	def _terminate_and_close_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True,
07057	                                 context=None):
07058	    """Terminate one process of this round and close its pipes.
07059	
07060	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
07061	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed.  The
07062	    method never raises: it is the last step of a failure and it never
07063	    replaces the failure that is on the way out.
07064	
07065	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: a
07066	    recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed
07067	    number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
07068	
07069	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
07070	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
07071	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
07072	    it and it is never closed under it.
07073	
07074	    The report names every step that did not succeed, so that the
07075	    caller can keep the original reference of a resource that is still
07076	    open.
07077	    """
07078	    return _release_launch_process(process, deadline=deadline,
07079	                                   close_pipes=close_pipes,
07080	                                   context=context)['failures']
07081	
07082	
07083	def _adopt_broker_process(process, *, channel_factory, executable,
07084	                          executable_sha256, deadline=None, aggregate=None,
07085	                          custody=None):
07086	    """Take the original process of the broker into the custody of a channel.
07087	
07088	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
07089	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
07090	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
07091	    nobody owns.
07092	
07093	    The **channel** is the only owner of the files of the process.  A
07094	    channel that exists and that could not be handed over is closed by
07095	    the launcher through the channel and never by closing the standard
07096	    input and the standard output of the process by itself: a file that
07097	    is closed under a live worker of the channel is a file that a
07098	    worker is reading from.
07099	
07100	    The **aggregate** of the launch is the owner that the outer code
07101	    uses: a partial channel - a channel whose reader thread did not
07102	    start - is found through the aggregate and never through a local
07103	    variable that is ``None`` at that moment.
07104	
07105	    A resource that could not be released keeps its **original**
07106	    reference in the table of the launcher: the session never received
07107	    the process and the round never loses the ownership of it.
07108	
07109	    The handover happens exactly once: the process that the channel
07110	    received is the process that the parent owns.
07111	    """
07112	    table = custody if custody is not None else _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY
07113	    launch = aggregate if aggregate is not None else _LaunchAggregate(
07114	        custody=table)
07115	    launch.bind_process(process)
07116	    # The launch is registered with the table of the launcher: the
07117	    # recovery of the table coordinates the resources of the launch in
07118	    # the dependency order - the channel before the process.
07119	    table.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
07120	    table.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
07121	    channel = None
07122	    try:
07123	        channel = channel_factory()
07124	        if channel is not None:
07125	            launch.bind_channel(channel)
07126	        broker = BrokerProcess(process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
07127	                               context=launch.context)
07128	    except BaseException as error:
07129	        # The resources of the launch are coordinated by the aggregate:
07130	        # the channel first and the process after it.  The resources
07131	        # that could not be released keep their original reference and
07132	        # their owner.
07133	        try:
07134	            launch.release(deadline=deadline)
07135	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as cancellation:
07136	            # The cancellation of the release never replaces the
07137	            # original failure of the launch: the original object
07138	            # continues to propagate and the cancellation stays
07139	            # **reachable** next to it.  The primary failure and the
07140	            # cancellation of the recovery coexist.
07141	            try:
07142	                error.lvw_cancelled = cancellation
07143	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07144	                pass
07145	        except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07146	            pass
07147	        raise
07148	    # The handover happened: the launcher does not own the process of
07149	    # the round any more.
07150	    table.forget(process)
07151	    table.forget(channel)
07152	    table.forget(launch)
07153	    launch.forget(channel)
07154	    launch.forget(process)
07155	    return broker
07156	
07157	
07158	def _check_launch_budget(context, stage):
07159	    """Check the budget of the round before a side effect of a launch.
07160	
07161	    The check is asked **before** the first side effect of the launch
07162	    - the creation of the directory, the writing of the plan and the
07163	    creation of the process - and it is asked **again** after the
07164	    preparatory work and before the next effect.  A launch that starts
07165	    after the deadline of the round arrived never creates a process.
07166	
07167	    The **original** context of the round is the authority: the
07168	    launcher never keeps a copy of the deadline and it never opens a
07169	    budget of its own.
07170	    """
07171	    if context is None:
07172	        return True
07173	    if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
07174	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
07175	    deadline = context.absolute_deadline
07176	    if deadline is not None and context.clock() >= deadline:
07177	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
07178	    return True
07179	
07180	
07181	def launch_broker(plan, run_root, nonce, *, context=None):
07182	    """Start the broker process of one round and keep its original handle.
07183	
07184	    The launcher is a Windows only operation.  It refuses on every other
07185	    platform before anything is started: a broker that cannot be started
07186	    on Windows is never replaced by an unconstrained process and never by
07187	    a simulation that pretends to be one.
07188	
07189	    The nonce and the plan are checked **before** the first directory and
07190	    the first file of the round exist, so that a refused launch leaves
07191	    nothing behind.
07192	
07193	    The ONE context of the round is held **before** the first side
07194	    effect of the launch: the creation of the process, the reading of
07195	    the executable, the channel and its endpoints all read the deadline
07196	    of the round from that context.  A launcher that waits for the end
07197	    of the launcher to bind the deadline would leave a window in which
07198	    the process of the round runs without a budget.
07199	
07200	    The **aggregate** of the launch is created before the process and it
07201	    holds every original resource of the launch: a failure at any point
07202	    of the launch finds the partial owner of the launch through it.
07203	    """
07204	    if os.name != 'nt':
07205	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLATFORM_UNSUPPORTED')
07206	    if plan is None:
07207	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID')
07208	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
07209	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
07210	    # The aggregate of this launch and the deadline of the round are
07211	    # taken **before** the first side effect of the launch.
07212	    launch = _LaunchAggregate(custody=_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY, context=context)
07213	    launch.bind_context(context)
07214	    # The table of the launcher reads the deadline of the round from
07215	    # the same context: a recovery of the launcher never opens a fresh
07216	    # budget of its own.
07217	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
07218	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
07219	    deadline = None
07220	    if context is not None:
07221	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
07222	    root = Path(run_root)
07223	    raw = plan.sealed_bytes()
07224	    digest = plan.sealed_sha256()
07225	    directory = root / 'temp' / 'native'
07226	    # The budget of the round is checked **before** the first side
07227	    # effect of the launch: a launch that starts after the deadline of
07228	    # the round arrived creates no directory and no file.
07229	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_DIRECTORY')
07230	    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
07231	    plan_path = directory / 'custody-plan.json'
07232	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_PLAN')
07233	    with plan_path.open('xb') as stream:
07234	        stream.write(raw)
07235	    command = build_broker_command(plan_path=plan_path, plan_sha256=digest,
07236	                                   run_root=root, nonce=nonce)
07237	    # The budget is checked **again** after the preparatory work and
07238	    # before the creation of the process: a plan file that consumed the
07239	    # whole lease of the round never produces a process.
07240	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_POPEN')
07241	    process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
07242	                               stderr=None, close_fds=True)
07243	    # The original process of the round is with the launcher from the
07244	    # moment of its creation.  The window between the creation of the
07245	    # process and the handover of the channel is not a window without an
07246	    # owner: a cancellation of the reading of the executable leaves the
07247	    # process with its owner and it never disappears.
07248	    launch.bind_process(process)
07249	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
07250	    executable = Path(command[0])
07251	    try:
07252	        executable_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
07253	    except OSError:
07254	        executable_sha256 = None
07255	    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
07256	        # The cancellation of the reading of the executable is cleaned
07257	        # up and it continues to propagate.  The process keeps its owner
07258	        # in the table of the launcher and the recovery of the launcher
07259	        # is the entry that releases it.
07260	        raise
07261	
07262	    def channel_factory():
07263	        """Build the channel of this launch with the context of the round.
07264	
07265	        The channel registers itself with the **aggregate** of the
07266	        launch before it builds anything: a channel whose reader
07267	        thread did not start is found by the outer owner through the
07268	        aggregate and the files of the process are never closed by the
07269	        process helper.
07270	        """
07271	        channel = StreamChannel(process.stdout, process.stdin,
07272	                                custody=launch, context=context)
07273	        if deadline is not None:
07274	            channel.bind_deadline(deadline)
07275	        return channel
07276	
07277	    return _adopt_broker_process(
07278	        process,
07279	        channel_factory=channel_factory,
07280	        executable=str(executable), executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
07281	        deadline=deadline, aggregate=launch)
07282	
07283	
07284	def read_sealed_plan(path, digest):
07285	    """Read one sealed plan document and check its digest."""
07286	    target = Path(path)
07287	    if not target.is_file():
07288	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_MISSING')
07289	    with target.open('rb') as stream:
07290	        raw = stream.read(MAX_PLAN_BYTES + 1)
07291	    if len(raw) > MAX_PLAN_BYTES:
07292	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_TOO_LARGE')
07293	    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest:
07294	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_DRIFT')
07295	    try:
07296	        document = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'))
07297	    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as error:
07298	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID') from error
07299	    return document
07300	
07301	
07302	def main(argv=None):
07303	    """The production entry of the broker.
07304	
07305	    The entry is fixed: one sealed plan, its digest, the run root of the
07306	    round and the nonce of the session.  The plan is validated again here,
07307	    because the broker never trusts a document that it did not check.
07308	    """
07309	    import argparse
07310	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
07311	    parser.add_argument('--plan', required=True)
07312	    parser.add_argument('--plan-sha256', required=True)
07313	    parser.add_argument('--run-root', required=True)
07314	    parser.add_argument('--nonce', required=True)
07315	    arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
07316	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(arguments.nonce)):
07317	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_NONCE_INVALID\n')
07318	        sys.stderr.flush()
07319	        return 2
07320	    document = read_sealed_plan(arguments.plan, arguments.plan_sha256)
07321	    try:
07322	        plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
07323	    except CustodyError as error:
07324	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
07325	        sys.stderr.flush()
07326	        return 2
07327	    if plan.sealed_sha256() != arguments.plan_sha256:
07328	        # The seal of the plan that the broker validated is not the seal
07329	        # that the parent wrote: the document drifted on its way here.
07330	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_PLAN_SEAL_DRIFT\n')
07331	        sys.stderr.flush()
07332	        return 2
07333	    try:
07334	        win32 = WindowsApi()
07335	    except BrokerError as error:
07336	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
07337	        sys.stderr.flush()
07338	        return 2
07339	    core = BrokerCore(win32=win32, plan=plan, run_root=arguments.run_root)
07340	    loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=None, plan=plan,
07341	                      nonce=arguments.nonce)
07342	    # The **absolute deadline** of the round is fixed and bound to the
07343	    # core **before** the transport of the round exists.  The reader of
07344	    # the channel and the workers of the endpoints therefore never
07345	    # start with an unbound context: the child has no window in which
07346	    # a read of the channel runs without a deadline.
07347	    deadline = loop.fix_deadline()
07348	    core.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
07349	    # The ONE context of the round is handed to the transport of the
07350	    # channel and to its endpoints: the channel, the endpoints and the
07351	    # workers of the endpoints read the same absolute deadline and none
07352	    # of them keeps a budget of its own.
07353	    transport = StreamChannel(sys.stdin.buffer, sys.stdout.buffer, api=win32,
07354	                              context=core.context)
07355	    transport.bind_deadline(deadline)
07356	    loop.bind_transport(transport)
07357	    try:
07358	        return loop.run()
07359	    finally:
07360	        # The round releases its own resources in the loop; the entry only
07361	        # closes the channel and never swallows a failure of the round.
07362	        try:
07363	            transport.close()
07364	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
07365	            pass
07366	
07367	
07368	if __name__ == '__main__':
07369	    sys.exit(main())
